Modified Function Code:
from re_arc.dsl import *
def generate_228f6490() -> dict:
    diff_lb = 0
    diff_ub = 1
    print(f"diff_lb: {diff_lb}, diff_ub: {diff_ub}")

    cols = interval(0, 10, 1)
    print(f"cols: {cols}")

    h = unifint(diff_lb, diff_ub, (10, 30))
    print(f"h: {h}")

    w = unifint(diff_lb, diff_ub, (10, 30))
    print(f"w: {w}")

    nsq = unifint(diff_lb, diff_ub, (1, (h * w) // 50))
    print(f"nsq: {nsq}")

    succ = 0
    tr = 0
    maxtr = 5 * nsq
    print(f"maxtr: {maxtr}")

    bgc = choice(cols)
    print(f"bgc: {bgc}")

    remcols = remove(bgc, cols)
    print(f"remcols: {remcols}")

    sqc = choice(remcols)
    print(f"sqc: {sqc}")

    gi = canvas(bgc, (h, w))
    print(f"gi: {gi}")

    inds = asindices(gi)
    print(f"inds: {inds}")

    forbidden = []
    while tr < maxtr and succ < nsq:
        tr += 1
        print(f"tr: {tr}")

        oh = randint(3, 6)
        print(f"oh: {oh}")

        ow = randint(3, 6)
        print(f"ow: {ow}")

        bd = asindices(canvas(-1, (oh, ow)))
        print(f"bd: {bd}")

        bounds = shift(asindices(canvas(-1, (oh-2, ow-2))), (1, 1))
        print(f"bounds: {bounds}")

        obj = {choice(totuple(bounds))}
        print(f"obj: {obj}")

        ncells = randint(1, (oh-2) * (ow-2))
        print(f"ncells: {ncells}")

        for k in range(ncells - 1):
            obj.add(choice(totuple((bounds - obj) & mapply(dneighbors, obj))))
        print(f"obj after adding cells: {obj}")

        sqcands = sfilter(inds, lambda ij: ij[0] <= h - oh and ij[1] <= w - ow)
        print(f"sqcands: {sqcands}")

        if len(sqcands) == 0:
            continue

        loc = choice(totuple(sqcands))
        print(f"loc: {loc}")

        bdplcd = shift(bd, loc)
        print(f"bdplcd: {bdplcd}")

        if bdplcd.issubset(inds):
            tmpinds = inds - bdplcd
            inobjn = normalize(obj)
            print(f"inobjn: {inobjn}")

            oh, ow = shape(obj)
            print(f"oh, ow: {oh}, {ow}")

            inobjcands = sfilter(inds, lambda ij: ij[0] <= h - oh and ij[1] <= w - ow)
            print(f"inobjcands: {inobjcands}")

            if len(inobjcands) == 0:
                continue

            loc2 = choice(totuple(inobjcands))
            print(f"loc2: {loc2}")

            inobjplcd = shift(inobjn, loc2)
            print(f"inobjplcd: {inobjplcd}")

            bdnorm = bd - obj
            print(f"bdnorm: {bdnorm}")

            if inobjplcd.issubset(tmpinds) and bdnorm not in forbidden and inobjn not in forbidden:
                forbidden.append(bdnorm)
                forbidden.append(inobjn)
                succ += 1
                print(f"succ: {succ}")

                inds = (inds - (bdplcd | inobjplcd)) - mapply(dneighbors, inobjplcd)
                print(f"inds after update: {inds}")

                col = choice(remcols)
                print(f"col: {col}")

                oplcd = shift(obj, loc)
                print(f"oplcd: {oplcd}")

                gi = fill(gi, sqc, bdplcd - oplcd)
                print(f"gi after fill: {gi}")

    nremobjs = unifint(diff_lb, diff_ub, (0, len(inds) // 25))
    print(f"nremobjs: {nremobjs}")

    succ = 0
    tr = 0
    maxtr = 10 * nremobjs
    print(f"maxtr: {maxtr}")

    while tr < maxtr and succ < nremobjs:
        tr += 1
        print(f"tr: {tr}")

        oh = randint(1, 4)
        print(f"oh: {oh}")

        ow = randint(1, 4)
        print(f"ow: {ow}")

        bounds = asindices(canvas(-1, (oh, ow)))
        print(f"bounds: {bounds}")

        obj = {choice(totuple(bounds))}
        print(f"obj: {obj}")

        ncells = randint(1, oh * ow)
        print(f"ncells: {ncells}")

        for k in range(ncells - 1):
            obj.add(choice(totuple((bounds - obj) & mapply(dneighbors, obj))))
        obj = normalize(obj)
        print(f"obj after normalization: {obj}")

        if obj in forbidden:
            continue

        cands = sfilter(inds, lambda ij: ij[0] <= h - oh and ij[1] <= w - ow)
        print(f"cands: {cands}")

        if len(cands) == 0:
            continue

        loc = choice(totuple(cands))
        print(f"loc: {loc}")

        plcd = shift(obj, loc)
        print(f"plcd: {plcd}")

        if plcd.issubset(inds):
            succ += 1
            print(f"succ: {succ}")

            inds = (inds - plcd) - mapply(dneighbors, plcd)
            print(f"inds after update: {inds}")

            col = choice(remcols)
            print(f"col: {col}")

            gi = fill(gi, col, plcd)
            print(f"gi after fill: {gi}")

    return {'input': gi}
